### 3004. [accelerate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accelerate) *(verb)* ^w-3004

### 3006. [accessible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accessible) *(adjective)* ^w-3006
- **UK:** `/əkˈsesəbl/` · **US:** `/əkˈsesəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/acces/accessible__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/acces/accessible__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/əˈkɒmədeɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accom/accommodate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accom/accommodate__us_1.mp3)

### 3008. [accumulate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accumulate) *(verb)* ^w-3008
- **UK:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__us_1.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - We have accumulated a great amount of evidence.

### 3009. [accurate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accurate) *(adjective)* ^w-3009
- **UK:** `/ˈækjərət/` · **US:** `/ˈækjərət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accur/accurate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accur/accurate__us_1.mp3)

### 3010. [acute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/acute) *(adjective)* ^w-3010
- **UK:** `/əˈkjuːt/` · **US:** `/əˈkjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acu/acute/acute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acu/acute/acute__us_1.mp3)

  - acute pain

### 3011. [adhere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adhere) *(verb)* ^w-3011
- **UK:** `/ədˈhɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ədˈhɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पालन करना, समर्थन करना, पालन
- **Examples:**
  - Once in the bloodstream, the bacteria adhere to the surface of the red cells.
  - Clean the surface first, or the paint will not adhere.
  - There was oil adhering to the bird’s feathers.
  - The tiles may not adhere properly if you do not use the correct glue.

### 3012. [adjacent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adjacent) *(adjective)* ^w-3012
- **UK:** `/əˈdʒeɪsnt/` · **US:** `/əˈdʒeɪsnt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adj/adjac/adjacent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adj/adjac/adjacent__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निकटवर्ती, लगा हुआ, पास
- **Examples:**
  - The planes landed on adjacent runways.
  - numbers that are adjacent in the sequence
  - adjacent to something Our farm land was adjacent to the river.
  - The vineyards of Verzy lie adjacent to those of Verzenay.

### 3023. [allege](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allege) *(verb)* ^w-3023
- **UK:** `/əˈledʒ/` · **US:** `/əˈledʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/all/alleg/allege__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/all/alleg/allege__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बिना सबूत के आरोप लगाना, आरोप लगाना, आरोप  लगाना
- **Examples:**

  - it is alleged (that)… It is alleged that he mistreated the prisoners.
  - be alleged to be, have, etc. something He is alleged to have mistreated the prisoners.
  - allege something This procedure should be followed in cases where dishonesty has been alleged.

### 3024. [allocate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allocate) *(verb)* ^w-3024

- **Meaning:** निर्धारित करना, नियत  करना, नियत करना
- **Examples:**

  - allocate something (to somebody/something) They intend to allocate more places to mature students this year.
  - More resources are being allocated to the project.
  - allocate somebody/something sth The project is being allocated more resources.

- **Meaning:** रोकना, निरोध करना, बाधा डालना

### 3702. [notify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notify) *(verb)* ^w-3702
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notif/notify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notif/notify__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सूचित करना, सूचना देना
- **Examples:**
  - notify somebody Competition winners will be notified by post.
  - You must notify us in writing if you wish to cancel your subscription.
  - notify somebody of something The police must be notified of the date of the demonstration.
  - notify something to somebody The date of the demonstration must be notified to the police.

### 3703. [notorious](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notorious) *(adjective)* ^w-3703

- **Meaning:** कुख्यात, बदनाम
- **Examples:**

### 3991. [recognize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recognize) *(verb)* ^w-3991
- **UK:** `/ˈrekəɡnaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈrekəɡnaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recog/recognize__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recog/recognize__us_3.mp3)

- **Examples:**
  - recognize somebody/something I recognized him as soon as he came in the room.
  - Do you recognize this tune?
  - You might not recognize the name but you'll know her face.
  - I recognized the voice immediately.
